Method: 提出一个贝叶斯控制框架，将脉冲动力学与概率推断集成在一起，用于自适应控制。使用山地车停车问题作为基准测试，该问题涉及非线性动力学。控制器通过脉冲驱动的动力学实现实时状态更新和目标导向行动计划的生成。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的控制器能够在实时条件下成功更新状态，并能够通过脉冲驱动的动力学生成目标导向的行动计划，在具有非线性动力学的环境中有效运作。

Conclusion: 该模型展示了作为计算神经科学与概率控制理论之间桥梁的潜力，为开发结合大脑计算原理的鲁棒自适应控制系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a Bayesian control framework that integrates spike-based dynamics with probabilistic inference for adaptive control. Bayesian inference is widely regarded as a core computational principle of brain function, providing a normative framework for perception, decision-making, and learning under uncertainty. By combining a biologically inspired spiking neural model with Bayesian inference principles, we propose a brain-like control algorithm capable of operating in uncertain environments. We use the mountain car parking problem as a benchmark with non-linear dynamics. Our results demonstrate that the proposed controller can successfully update states in real time and generate goal-directed action plans through spike-driven dynamics. The results highlight the proposed model's potential as a bridge between computational neuroscience and probabilistic control theory.

</details>


### [85] [A Strong Linear Baseline for Whole-Heart Cardiac Shape Completion on CT, with an Open Eleven-Structure Statistical Shape Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19932)
*Matej Gazda,Jakub Gazda,Juraj Gazda,Peter Drotar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个基于383例自动标注CT的11结构心脏统计形状模型（11,571顶点对应），并系统比较了心脏结构补全的不同估计方法。在76例内部测试集上，闭式条件高斯估计器以3.717mm的平均逐顶点误差显著优于掩码条件图变分自编码器（5.248mm）和最近邻检索（8.931mm），配对差异为1.531mm（95%置信区间1.384-1.711）。该优势在外部58例及第二公共基准20例中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共心脏数据集中不同来源的标注子集不一致（缺乏共享对应关系），无法直接合并用于统计分析；已发布的心脏形状资源中没有一个将心耳、肺静脉和腔静脉残端作为独立网格块包含在单一网格中；且现有补全基准是将深度模型与形状模态的最小二乘投影比较，而非同一拟合模型隐含的条件估计器，评价标准不够严谨。

Method: 构建了基于383例自动标注CT的11结构统计形状模型（含心耳、肺静脉和腔静脉残端作独立块），在11,571顶点对应关系下建立。在固定内部划分和评估端点下，比较三种补全估计器：闭式条件高斯估计器、五次重拟合的掩码条件图变分自编码器、以及最近邻检索。评估包括内部76例逐一、三种缺失结构数量设置、以及外部58例CT和20例公共基准的外部验证。

Result: 闭式条件高斯估计器在内部76例测试中达到3.717mm平均逐顶点误差（对1、3、5、9个观测结构等权平均），显著优于图变分自编码器的5.248mm和最近邻检索的8.931mm；配对差1.531mm（95%CI 1.384-1.711）。在外部58例中仅5个结构可评分，闭式估计器在平均表面距离、95百分位Hausdorff距离和Chamfer误差上均更低。在20例公共基准中，4个补全结构中有3个可评分且排序一致。

Conclusion: 发布的11结构心脏CT统计形状模型及其条件补全算子支持对齐CT上的队列统一化研究，但不面向临床应用。闭式条件高斯估计器在心脏结构补全上系统性地优于深度生成模型和检索方法，表明对学习到的形状模态直接使用其隐含的条件估计可提供更优的重建性能。

Abstract: Public cardiac cohorts annotate different subsets of the heart, so shapes from separate sources cannot be pooled without shared correspondence. Among released cardiac shape resources, none we identified carries the atrial appendage, pulmonary veins, and caval stumps as separate blocks in one mesh. Completion benchmarks also compare deep models against a least-squares projection onto shape modes, not the conditional estimator the same fitted model implies. We release an eleven- structure cardiac computed-tomography (CT) statistical shape model, built from 383 automatically labelled cases in 11 571-vertex correspondence, and compare completion estimators under one frozen internal split and endpoint. On a 76-case internal list held out from fitting, a closed-form conditional-Gaussian estimator reconstructed the missing non-chamber structures at 3.717 mm mean per-vertex error, averaged equally over one, three, five, and nine observed structures. A five-refit mask-conditioned graph variational autoencoder reached 5.248 mm and nearest-neighbour retrieval 8.931 mm. The paired difference was 1.531 mm (95% confidence interval 1.384 to 1.711), and the ordering held in a raw-coordinate sensitivity arm. Expert manual labels exist for 58 external CT cases, but our registered reference is close enough to score only five structures. There the closed-form estimator again had lower average surface distance, 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance, and Chamfer error for both completed atria. On a second public benchmark of 20 cases the reference was close enough for three of four completed structures, and the same ordering held there. Four structures have no expert reference. The released model and its completion operator support cohort-unification research on aligned CT, not clinical use.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Early-to-Final Solution Consistency for MILP Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19953)
*Guanlin Li,Chengrui Gao,Chenguang Wang,Haopu Shang,Zherong Zhang,Ke Xue,Jixiang Lu,Weiyong Yang,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的求解器感知范式，将混合整数线性规划（MILP）的解决学习目标从直接预测变量赋值转变为预测早期搜索解与全预算解的一致性。通过固定被认为一致的赋值来指导下游搜索，并在推理时集成多个早期解的一致性预测以提高鲁棒性。在四个MILP基准上，使用Gurobi将原始间隙平均降低56.9%，并在组合拍卖实例上完全闭合间隙；Gurobi训练的模型可零样本迁移至SCIP，平均间隙降低36.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法试图从实例级静态特征（如变量-约束二部图）直接预测高质量解，但仅从实例特征准确预测解十分困难，且这些方法忽视了求解器搜索过程中揭示的信息。作者发现MILP求解器早期搜索阶段产生的解（计算成本低）与全预算搜索后的解在结构上往往很接近，因此希望利用这一观测改进学习方法。

Method: 提出solver-informed范式，将学习目标从变量赋值转移为"早期到最终一致性"（early-to-final consistency）：对每个变量预测其早期赋值是否应保留在全预算解中。预测的一致性用于指导下游搜索，例如固定被认为一致的变量赋值。推理时将多个早期解的一致性预测进行集成以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个MILP基准上，提出的方法在多种下游流程中均改善了预测引导搜索的效果。使用Gurobi时，原始间隙平均降低56.9%，在组合拍卖实例上完全闭合间隙。Gurobi训练的模型零样本迁移到SCIP无需调整，基准上平均间隙降低36.4%。

Conclusion: 通过利用求解器搜索早期阶段的信息来预测变量赋值的一致性，而非直接预测解本身，可以更有效地利用学习模型引导搜索，显著提升MILP求解效率，且具有良好的跨求解器迁移能力。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental problem class in operations research and combinatorial optimization, with broad applications to industrial decision-making. Owing to their NP-hardness, however, modern solvers may struggle to find high-quality solutions for challenging MILP instances within practical time limits. Recent learning-based approaches seek to accelerate MILP solving by directly predicting high-quality solutions from static instance-level features, such as variable-constraint bipartite graphs. Yet accurate solution prediction from instance features alone is difficult, and these methods largely overlook the information revealed during the solver's search process. In this paper, we find that solutions produced at the early search stage of MILP solvers, which are computationally cheap to obtain, are often structurally close to the solutions found after full-budget search. Motivated by this observation, we propose a new solver-informed paradigm that shifts the learning target from variable assignment to early-to-final consistency: for each variable, we predict whether its early-stage assignment should persist in full-budget solutions. The predicted consistency naturally guides downstream search, for instance by fixing the assignments deemed consistent. At inference time, we further ensemble consistency predictions across multiple early-stage solutions to improve robustness. Experiments across four MILP benchmarks show our method improves prediction-guided search across diverse downstream pipelines. With Gurobi, our proposed method reduces the primal gap by 56.9% on average and closes it completely on combinatorial auction instances. Besides, we transferred the Gurobi-trained model zero-shot to SCIP without adaptation, achieving a 36.4% average gap reduction across benchmarks.

</details>


### [87] [Rethinking Patch Based Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Semantic Structured Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19966)
*Jiazhe Wang,Zhiquan Huang,Linjing Xue,Ming Liu,Meiwen Li,Ruijuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCPaT，一种基于语义结构化分区的Transformer框架，通过自适应语义单元生成、动态语义图构建和重要性感知路由机制，解决多变量时间序列预测中现有分区方法破坏语义边界、引入冗余表征及缺乏语义结构组织的问题。在12个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分区的MTSF方法存在三类局限：固定分区破坏有意义的时间边界；多尺度分区引入跨尺度冗余表示；可扩展分区虽提升灵活性但缺乏显式机制来组织语义结构和建模异构时间模式间的交互。这些问题的根源在于缺乏语义层面的结构组织能力。

Method: SCPaT包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应语义单元生成——将输入序列分解为语义一致的时间单元；(2) 动态语义图构建——建模语义单元间的有向依赖关系并组织成高阶语义块；(3) 重要性感知路由机制——根据语义块的重要程度自适应地将其调度到不同专家进行定制化建模。

Result: 在12个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验验证了SCPaT的有效性，表明其在MTSF任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SCPaT通过语义结构化分区为MTSF提供了一种新的建模范式，弥补了现有分区方法在语义组织上的不足，证明以语义为导向的分解与路由机制能有效提升多变量时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting (MTSF) is a fundamental task in many real world applications. Existing patch based forecasting methods generally fall into three categories: fixed partitioning, multi-scale partitioning, and extendable partitioning. Fixed partitioning often breaks meaningful temporal boundaries, multi-scale partitioning may introduce redundant representations across scales, and extendable partitioning improves flexibility but still lacks an explicit mechanism for organizing semantic structure and modeling interactions among heterogeneous temporal patterns. To address these limitations, we propose SCPaT, a Transformer based framework built on semantic structured partitioning. SCPaT first decomposes input sequences into semantically consistent units through adaptive semantic unit generation, then constructs a dynamic semantic graph to model directed dependencies among these units and organize them into higher order semantic blocks. Based on these structured representations, an importance aware routing mechanism adaptively dispatches different semantic blocks to different experts for customized modeling. Extensive experiments on 12 real world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SCPaT.

</details>


### [88] [ReguSim: Evaluating LLM Agent Rule Grounding in Financial Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19974)
*Yiyang Luo,Yihang Jiang,Qijun Xie,Liang Lan,Lin Willian Cong,Anyi Rao,Yunya Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReguSim可控金融合规环境和ReguBench目标标记监控基准，将金融LLM智能体合规能力拆分为四个构件：陈述推理、尝试行动、执行强制和监控证据。实验发现可见规则只能减少而非消除违规行动，交易员推理可能误导独立监控者，而简单结构化基线在监控任务上不逊于或优于纯提示LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场的LLM智能体即使能引用规则，仍可能提交违反可执行约束的订单或误读监管证据。现有评估缺乏对合规问题各环节的细粒度分离，需要将金融合规评估从单一合规分数转向对规则约束行动和证据使用的审计。

Method: 构建ReguSim受控金融合规环境，模拟交易员与监控者的交互；构建ReguBench目标标记基准，将评估分离为四个独立构件：陈述推理（stated reasoning）、尝试行动（attempted action）、执行强制（execution enforcement）和监控证据（monitor evidence）。在DeepSeek V4 Pro和Gemini 3.5 Flash上进行交易员实验，并通过桥接研究发现交易员理由对监控者的影响。

Result: 可见规则仅减少而未消除被拒绝的行动；激励或角色框架会改变行为；交易员推理在缺乏执行证据时能误导独立监控者；简单结构化基线在监控任务上达到或超过仅提示的LLM。

Conclusion: 金融合规评估不应依赖单一合规分数，而应框架化为对规则锚定行动和证据使用的审计，强调区分推理、行动、执行和证据四个环节对评估可信度的重要性。

Abstract: LLM agents in financial markets may cite rules yet still submit orders that violate executable constraints or misread surveillance evidence. We introduce ReguSim, a controlled financial-compliance environment, and ReguBench, a target-marked monitoring benchmark, to separate four artifacts: stated reasoning, attempted action, execution enforcement, and monitor evidence. In trader runs with DeepSeek V4 Pro and Gemini 3.5 Flash, visible rules reduce but do not eliminate rejected actions, and incentive or persona framing shifts behavior. A bridge study shows that trader rationales can mislead an independent monitor unless enforcement evidence is shown. In monitoring, simple structured baselines either match or exceed prompt-only LLMs. The results frame financial compliance evaluation as an audit of rule-grounded actions and evidence use, rather than a single compliance score.

</details>


### [89] [Optimal Skill Selection for LLM Agents with Provable Bicriteria Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19993)
*Yu Chen,Ruishuo Chen,Xun Wang,Zhuoran Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将技能选择形式化为一个受硬令牌预算约束的优化问题，即最大化单调子模效益减去上下文惩罚，并提出多项式时间算法BPS，实现了(1-1/e, 1)双准则近似保证，在BigCodeBench变体上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过将可复用的技能文档加载进有界上下文窗口来获取任务特定能力，技能选择成为决定任务性能和令牌成本的首要因素。然而现有智能体仅基于语义相关性独立评分，通过top-k或贪婪打包方式组选技能集，缺乏质量保证和成本意识，导致冗余或不当技能浪费稀缺的上下文令牌甚至降低性能。

Method: 作者首次建立了所选技能集如何影响执行结果的模型，将技能选择形式化为优化问题：在硬令牌预算下选择技能集以最大化单调子模效益减去上下文惩罚。针对此问题提出最佳前缀选择（BPS）算法，这是一个多项式时间算法，并证明了技能选择领域首个性能保证——双准则(1-1/e, 1)近似，其中效益系数在多项式时间内是最优的。

Result: 在受污染控制的BigCodeBench变体上，BPS在所有基线上表现最佳，达到0.73的实测任务成功率，而发布的技能路由器、文本检索器和执行器自身选择仅为0.20-0.52。同时，BPS比最强发布路由器少使用28%的令牌。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上奠基了技能选择的优化框架，提供了首个带性能保证的算法，并在实证上证明BPS在任务成功率和令牌效率上均大幅超越现有方法，表明将技能选择视为预算约束下的集合优化问题具有重要实践价值。

Abstract: Loading reusable skill documents into a bounded context window is now the primary way large language model (LLM) agents acquire task-specific capabilities, which makes skill selection a first-order determinant of task performance and token cost. Yet current agents score skills independently by semantic relevance and assemble the set by top-$k$ or greedy packing, with no quality guarantee or cost awareness on the selected set. As a result, redundant or poorly chosen skills waste scarce context tokens and can even degrade performance. We give the first model of how the selected skill set shapes execution outcomes and cast skill selection as an optimization problem: choose a skill set under a hard token budget to maximize a monotone submodular benefit minus context penalty. For this problem, we develop Best Prefix Selection (BPS), a polynomial-time algorithm, and prove, to our knowledge, the first performance guarantee for skill selection: a bicriteria $(1-1/e,1)$ approximation whose benefit coefficient is optimal in polynomial time. On a contamination-controlled BigCodeBench variant, BPS outperforms all the baselines, reaching $0.73$ measured task success versus $0.20$--$0.52$ for released skill routers, text retrievers, and the executor's own selection, on $28\%$ fewer tokens than the strongest released router.

</details>


### [90] [ExPhy: A Benchmark for Explicit Physical Property Learning in Multi-Object Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20009)
*Rui Wang,Yeteng Wu,Xianlin Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExPhy，一个包含 24,000 个模拟物理场景的多物体轨迹预测基准，显式标注物体级质量、摩擦力和恢复系数等物理属性，并配套提出 PhyODE 物理引导模型，在 OOD-Initial 长时程设置下相比最强基线将 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 33.1% 和 31.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测基准很少将物体级物理属性作为显式评估目标与轨迹预测联合考察，导致难以判断模型是否真正捕获了支配运动的物理规律，而不仅仅是在拟合轨迹模式。

Method: 构建 ExPhy 基准：包含 24,000 个模拟物理场景，提供质量、摩擦力、恢复系数的物体级标注，设有 ID 划分及两种 OOD 划分（OOD-Parameter 与 OOD-Initial）；同时提出 PhyODE 模型，通过显式属性接口从观测轨迹估计物理属性，并利用估计结果进行可微分的未来轨迹展开。

Result: 在长时程 OOD-Initial 设置下，PhyODE 相比最强基线将 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 33.1% 和 31.0%；在 ComPhy 上的零样本评估验证了跨基准迁移能力；属性级分析表明准确的轨迹预测并不必然意味着底层物理属性的准确恢复。

Conclusion: ExPhy 与 PhyODE 填补了轨迹预测与物理属性估计联合评估的空白，并揭示了两者之间可能存在的脱节问题，为物理感知的轨迹预测研究提供了新的评测基准与模型范式。

Abstract: Understanding object dynamics requires not only predicting future trajectories but also examining whether a model captures the physical properties that govern motion. However, existing benchmarks rarely expose object-level physical properties as explicit evaluation targets alongside trajectory forecasting. To address this gap, we introduce \emph{ExPhy}, a multi-object trajectory forecasting benchmark containing 24,000 simulated physical scenes with explicit object-level labels for mass, friction, and restitution. ExPhy provides observed and future trajectories together with an in-distribution (ID) split and two out-of-distribution (OOD) splits over physical parameters (OOD-Parameter) and initial states (OOD-Initial) for jointly evaluating trajectory forecasting and physical property estimation. We further instantiate \textsc{PhyODE}, a physics-guided model with an explicit property interface that estimates physical properties from observed trajectories and uses them for differentiable future rollout. On the long-horizon OOD-Initial setting, \textsc{PhyODE} reduces ADE and FDE by 33.1\% and 31.0\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Zero-shot evaluation on ComPhy further assesses cross-benchmark transfer. Property-level analyses reveal that accurate trajectory forecasting does not necessarily imply accurate recovery of the underlying physical properties. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Zest86/ExPhy.

</details>


### [91] [Manifold Drift in Flow Preference Optimization: A Root Cause of Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20011)
*Yansen Han,Shengyi Liao,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThermoDPO，一种温度控制的偏好优化方法，用于解决流匹配（flow matching）中偏好优化导致的"流形漂移"问题，即在奖励驱动更新时终端样本偏离预训练数据流形。理论证明偏好更新在终端位移存在非零法向分量时会脱离预训练流形，而最优流匹配可恢复终端数据分布。方法上通过锚定偏好样本的温度控制目标连接拒绝采样微调和FlowDPO，并加入加权变体增强低温度下的信号强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化已成为生成模型的标准对齐方法，但将其扩展到连续时间动力学（如流匹配）时，奖励驱动的更新会修改传输轨迹，且缺乏对预训练数据流形的固有约束，导致终端样本偏离预训练支撑集（称为流形漂移）。作者旨在从理论和实践两方面解决这一核心问题。

Method: 1) 形式上定义并理论分析流形漂移：证明最优流匹配能恢复终端数据分布，而偏好更新在终端位移有非零法向分量时会离开预训练流形。2) 提出ThermoDPO，一个温度控制的损失函数，将成对偏好优化锚定在偏好样本上；在不同温度区间内，该目标可退化为拒绝采样微调（RFT）和FlowDPO。3) 设计ThermoDPO-weighted加权变体，以缓解低温下信号衰减的问题。

Result: 在主要玩具基准上，ThermoDPO-weighted的StrictScore达到0.899，远超FlowDPO的0.629和FlowDPO+RFT的0.857。在SD3.5-M上（CFG=4.5），OCR指标提升47.5%，四个指标平均值提升16.0%。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析揭示了流匹配中偏好优化引发流形漂移的机理，并提出了有效的温度控制方案ThermoDPO及其加权变体，在基准测试中大幅超越现有方法，为连续时间生成模型的对齐问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Preference optimization is a standard alignment method for generative models, yet extending it to continuous-time dynamics remains non-trivial. In flow matching, reward-driven updates modify transport trajectories without an inherent constraint to the pretrained data manifold and can move terminal samples off the pretrained support. We formalize this failure mode as manifold drift. Theoretically, we show that optimal flow matching recovers the terminal data distribution, whereas a preference update leaves the pretrained manifold whenever its induced terminal displacement has a nonzero normal component. As a remedy, we propose ThermoDPO, a temperature-controlled objective that anchors pairwise preference optimization on preferred samples. Across temperature regimes, this objective connects rejection sampling fine-tuning and FlowDPO and controls a pointwise reconstruction-based surrogate for manifold distance. To counteract diminished signals at low temperatures, we further introduce a weighted variant, ThermoDPO-weighted. On the main toy benchmark, ThermoDPO-weighted attains a StrictScore of 0.899, compared with 0.629 for FlowDPO and 0.857 for FlowDPO+RFT. On SD3.5-M at CFG = 4.5, it improves OCR by 47.5% and the average of four metrics by 16.0%.

</details>


### [92] [Contrastive Mixed Prompt Learning for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Unseen Modality Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20019)
*Kaixin Xu,NaiJin Liu,Yulin Kang,Tangyue Jin,Zixuan Yu,Wenxi Zhao,Yibei Liu,Qianle Zhang,Yangyang Wu,Mengying Zhu,Meng Xi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于解决"未见模态组合"的不完整多模态情感分析问题（IMSAUMC）的模型CMPL（对比混合提示学习），通过标签引导的对比特征学习、带软路由器的模态组合提示以及三种提示对比学习策略，在三个公开数据集上相比现有最优方法取得了超过5%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不完整多模态情感分析方法要么假设数据是随机缺失的，要么针对特定的缺失模式设计，忽视了训练和测试阶段模态组合不一致的问题。在实际场景中，测试阶段常常会遇到训练阶段未出现过的模态组合，导致模型泛化能力不足和性能不稳定。因此需要解决未见模态组合下的泛化问题。

Method: 提出CMPL模型，包含三个核心组件：（1）标签引导的对比特征学习机制，用于学习鲁棒且可区分的跨模态表示；（2）带有软路由器的模态组合提示设计，用于更好地学习多种模态组合；（3）三种提示对比学习策略，使模型能够有效学习对应未见模态组合的提示，从而增强在多样化测试场景下的泛化能力。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，CMPL相比现有最优方法在准确率上实现了超过5%的提升。

Conclusion: CMPL通过提示学习与对比学习的结合，有效解决了未见模态组合下的不完整多模态情感分析问题，显著提升了模型在训练和测试模态分布不一致场景下的泛化能力和性能稳定性。

Abstract: Incomplete multimodal sentiment analysis has garnered significant attention in recent years. Existing approaches typically assume that data is missing at random or are designed specifically for certain missing patterns, ignoring the modality combination inconsistency between training and testing phases. However, in real-world scenarios, the testing phase often encounters modal combinations that were not present during the training phase, which leads to insufficient generalization capabilities and unstable performance. In this paper, we introduce the problem of Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Unseen Modality Combinations (IMSAUMC), aiming to enhance model generalization for unseen modality combinations. To address this challenge, we propose the model named $\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{M}$ixed $\textbf{P}$rompt $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textsf{CMPL}$) for IMSAUMC. It introduces a label-guided contrastive feature learning mechanism to learn robust and discriminative cross-modal representations. Additionally, we design modality-combination prompts with a soft router to facilitate better learning of various modality combinations. Furthermore, we introduce three prompt contrastive learning strategies, which enable effective learning of prompts corresponding to unseen modality combinations, thereby significantly strengthening the model's generalization capabilities in diverse testing scenarios. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets demonstrate that $\textsf{CMPL}$ achieves more than a 5% improvement in accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [93] [A three-dimensional typology of agency for advanced AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20041)
*Willem Fourie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对前沿AI系统提出了一种三维度的能动性（agency）类型学框架，区分道德与法律能动性、个体与集体模式、人类与非人类主体，组合产生八种可能的能动性类型，并分为常规、争议性和有争议的类别，以解释非道德形式的AI能动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注AI能动性作为规范性概念以及高度agentic的AI系统，缺乏一个框架来回答先进AI系统所体现的能动性类型问题，尤其是当考虑非道德形式的能动性时。随着前沿AI系统在工具性目标追求中越来越难以将行动归因于特定人类行为者，区分法律与道德能动性变得日益重要。

Method: 基于哲学、伦理学、法律理论和社会学中的既有理论立场，构建了一个包含三个维度的能动性类型学：(1) 能动性的性质（道德的或法律的）；(2) 能动性的模式（个体的或集体的）；(3) 能动性的场所（人类的或非人类的）。通过三个维度的组合产生八种可能的能动性实例化，并将其分类为常规、有争议或争议性的类型。该类型学将法律能动性与道德能动性分离，从而为考虑个体性、法律性、非人类能动性创造了概念空间，而不预设先进AI系统是道德主体。

Result: 该框架成功地将法律与道德能动性加以区分，开辟了在不需要预设AI为道德主体的情况下讨论非人类法律能动性的概念空间。通过在工具性目标追求使得AI行动归因于特定人类行为者日益复杂化的背景下，提供了分析先进AI系统能动性类型的系统化工具。

Conclusion: 该类型学为分析前沿AI系统的能动性提供了系统的概念框架，特别是在非道德形式的能动性方面，并强调了在AI工具性目标追求日益复杂化的背景下，区分法律与道德能动性的现实意义。这一框架有助于未来研究更精确地探讨AI系统的能动性类型及其责任归属问题。

Abstract: Research on the agency of advanced artificial intelligence (AI) systems focuses on agency as a normative concept and on the agency of particularly agentic AI systems. While recent work also focuses on the different profiles of agentic systems, no framework exists to address the question of the type of agency instantiated by advanced AI systems, particularly when considering non-moral forms of agency. Based on established theoretical positions in philosophy, ethics, legal theory and sociology, we develop a typology of agency for frontier AI systems consisting of three dimensions: the nature of agency (moral or legal), its mode (individual or collective) and its locus (human or non-human). Combining these dimensions produces eight possible instantiations of agency, which we classify as conventional, contested or controversial. The typology separates legal from moral agency and thereby creates conceptual space for considering individual, legal, non-human agency without presupposing that advanced AI systems are moral agents. We argue that this distinction is increasingly relevant where instrumental goal pursuit complicates the attribution of AI actions to particular human actors.

</details>


### [94] [On the Applicability of Safety Nets: A Safety-By-Design Solution for Certifying Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20053)
*Johann Maximilian Christensen,Thomas Stefani,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对Safety Nets（神经网络压缩+查找表组合）进行系统性的性能分析与设计权衡研究，通过比较不同神经网络架构，确定3-5个隐藏层、每层50-100节点搭配one-hot编码为最优设计参数，可将系统体积缩小近三个数量级并保证100%正确的离散输入输出，满足EASA认证要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在航空安全关键系统中的整合面临认证和部署的巨大挑战。虽然Safety Nets已被提出作为满足EASA Safety-by-Design要求的方法，但此前缺乏对其性能特征和系统设计权衡的全面研究，导致无法确定最优设计参数。

Method: 系统性地比较多种不同架构的神经网络，分析神经网络与查找表尺寸之间的权衡关系，评估不同架构对存储/内存需求和认证合规性的影响，并在HCAS和VCAS两个场景下验证，提供首个开源实现。

Result: 3-5个隐藏层、每层50-100节点结合one-hot编码的架构达到最优平衡，神经网络能准确表示至少97%的数据，紧凑查找表处理剩余误差。Safety Nets整体将系统体积缩小近三个数量级，适配当前航空电子硬件内存预算，保证整个离散化输入空间100%正确输出。

Conclusion: Safety Nets是可行的Safety-by-Design解决方案，为航空领域可认证的AI系统提供了切实可行的路径，可推广至其他安全关键应用。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in safety-critical aviation systems presents significant challenges for certification and deployment. Aviation, often regarded as the safest form of transportation, relies on numerous safety-critical systems. For future safety-critical AI-based systems, EASA requires a Safety-by-Design approach, which can be achieved by using Safety Nets that combine neural network compression with lookup tables to ensure 100 % correct runtime behavior across the discretized operational design domain. Although Safety Nets have been studied, no comprehensive study of their performance characteristics and system design trade-offs has been conducted. This work presents the first systematic analysis of the trade-off between neural network and lookup table size in Safety Nets. By systematically comparing neural networks with diverse architectures, this study identifies optimal design parameters that minimize overall storage and memory requirements while maintaining certification compliance. Results demonstrate that architectures with 3 to 5 hidden layers, each with approximately 50 to 100 nodes, combined with one-hot encoding, achieve the best balance. In these configurations, neural networks accurately represent at least 97 % of the data, while compact lookup tables handle the remaining errors. The resulting Safety Nets reduce the system size by almost three orders of magnitude, fitting within the memory budget of current avionics hardware while guaranteeing 100 % correct outputs across the entire discretized input space, as required by EASA guidelines. This work provides the first-ever open-source implementation of Safety Nets for HCAS and VCAS with replicable results, demonstrating a practical pathway toward certifiable AI-based systems in aviation and establishing Safety Nets as a viable Safety-by-Design solution for safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [95] [What You Can't See Is What You Learn: Restricted Evidence Visibility Favors Compositional Generalization in Shared-Genome Language-Model Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20054)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多模块系统中限制证据可见性（restricted visibility）对梯度训练所发现解决方案的影响。通过冻结预训练语言模型加低秩适配器构成四细胞社会，仅通过固定中继中的两个连续向量通信。在自然语言函数组合任务上，受限社会在9/10的配对中比全局可见孪生模型高至少20点，中位配对优势分别为0.7648和0.6050。尽管受限可见性并非组合的必要条件，但它在该协议下显著提高了泛化中继的概率，并倾向于产生可复用的、以值为索引的接口。然而，预注册电池正式失败，因为受限臂的中位深度三准确率为0.6988，低于0.70的地板值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模块系统通常将完整输入暴露给每个模块。研究者希望探究限制证据可见性是否会改变梯度训练发现的解决方案，即受限可见性是否影响系统学习到的组合泛化能力与通信接口的性质。

Method: 采用四细胞社会架构，共享一个冻结的预训练语言模型和一个低秩适配器，仅通过两个模型宽度的连续向量在固定中继中通信。在密封的自然语言函数组合任务上，训练十对匹配的受限/全局配对，这些配对共享初始化字节、训练顺序、token布局、参数和计算，仅注意力掩码不同。通过通信消融、同值数据包移植、破坏性干预和反事实数据包等审计手段评估接口性质。

Result: 受限社会在9/10配对中以至少20点优势超越全局可见孪生模型，深度二和深度三的中位配对优势分别为0.7648和0.6050。切断通信使所有受限社会降至随机水平。深度三优势在未出现在训练中的组合函数上仍保持0.558。六个受限社会的同值数据包移植在0.94-1.00范围内保持行为一致性，破坏性干预使性能崩溃，反事实数据包将输出导向预期的数学答案。但唯一高性能全局模型的数据包不可跨情节互换。受限臂中位深度三准确率为0.6988，低于0.70的预注册地板值。

Conclusion: 受限可见性并非组合的必要条件，但在该协议下能大幅提高泛化中继被发现的概率，并倾向于产生可复用的、以值为索引的接口，而非全局模型中不可互换的接口。预注册电池因中位精度略低于0.70地板而正式失败。早期资格队列也显示0/10完全通过，所有模型在普通语言保持任务上失败，系统仅适用于显式任务门控的使用场景。

Abstract: Multi-module systems often expose every module to the full input. We test whether restricting evidence visibility changes which solutions gradient-based training discovers. Four-cell societies share one frozen pretrained language model and one low-rank adapter, communicating only through two model-width continuous vectors in a fixed relay. On a prospectively sealed natural-language function-composition task, we train ten matched restricted/global pairs sharing initialization bytes, training order, token layout, parameters, and computation; only the attention mask differs. Restricted societies outperform their globally visible twins by at least 20 points at both depths in 9 of 10 pairs, with median paired advantages of 0.7648 and 0.6050. Cutting communication reduces every restricted society to chance, and the depth-three advantage remains 0.558 on programs whose composite function never appeared in training. Across six audited restricted societies, same-value packet transplants preserve behavior at 0.94-1.00 across all tested interfaces; destructive interventions collapse performance; and counterfactual packets redirect outputs toward the mathematically predicted answer. The sole high-performing global model also requires communication, but its same-value packets are not interchangeable across episodes. Restricted visibility is thus not necessary for composition; under this protocol it substantially increases the probability of a generalizing relay and favors a reusable, value-indexed interface. The complete preregistered battery nevertheless formally fails because restricted-arm median depth-three accuracy is 0.6988, below the 0.70 floor. An earlier qualification cohort likewise yielded 0/10 complete passes: one model met every task-performance gate, but all ten failed ordinary-language preservation, confining the system to explicitly task-gated use.

</details>


### [96] [DECOWAM: Decoupled Whole-Body World-Action Model for Legged Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20114)
*Siyuan Ma,Boshi Zhang,Yutian Zhang,Qinglian Wu,Jiaqi Zhai,Dong Wei,Qiaojun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DECOWAM，一个全身世界-动作模型，通过条件接口分离相机自运动、基座动作与机械臂动作，并引入ARMDOG真实机器人数据集。实验表明DECOWAM在视频与动作预测上优于FastWAM（动作MSE降低21.7%，仅需25.95M可训练参数），在79次闭环试验中展现出最优的全身协调与基座位移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作要求机器人预测运动与机械臂动作如何联合改变未来观测并控制自身。现有世界-动作模型主要面向固定基座平台，未能显式区分相机自运动与基座/机械臂动作，缺乏全身（whole-body）建模能力。

Method: DECOWAM冻结了适配后的FastWAM骨干网络，并训练：(1) 残差适配器；(2) 从特权观测中蒸馏得到的动作等价未来瓶颈（action-equivalent future bottleneck）；(3) 对抗式分离的基座与机械臂隐变量；(4) 用于视频预测的基座速度条件。此外构建了ARMDOG数据集，同步记录视频、全身状态、动作与语言描述。

Result: 在固定回放协议下，DECOWAM在视频预测与动作预测上均优于FastWAM，动作MSE降低21.7%，仅使用25.95M可训练适配参数。在每种方法79次闭环实验中，DECOWAM达到最高的全身协调性和基座位移鲁棒性，任务完成率与最强基线持平。

Conclusion: 结果表明，具身感知的因子分解（embodiment-aware factorization）能够以参数高效的方式支持移动视角下的联合视觉预测与全身控制，验证了将相机自运动与机械臂/基座动作显式分离的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to predict how locomotion and arm motion jointly alter future observations and control. Existing world-action models, developed largely for fixed-base platforms, do not explicitly distinguish camera ego-motion from base and arm actions. Here we introduce DECOWAM, a whole-body world-action model that separates these factors through dedicated conditional interfaces. DECOWAM freezes an adapted FastWAM backbone and trains residual adapters, an action-equivalent future bottleneck distilled from privileged observations, adversarially separated base and arm latents, and base-velocity conditioning for video prediction. We further introduce ARMDOG, a real-robot dataset that synchronizes video, whole-body state and action, and language. On a fixed replay protocol, DECOWAM improved both future-video and action prediction over FastWAM, reducing action MSE by 21.7% with 25.95M trainable adaptation parameters. Across 79 closed-loop trials per method, it achieved the highest observed whole-body coordination and base-displacement robustness among the compared systems, while task completion remained comparable to the strongest baseline. These results show that embodiment-aware factorization can support parameter-efficient joint visual prediction and whole-body control under moving viewpoints.

</details>


### [97] [DARS: Dual-Level Credit Assignment RL with Structured Reasoning for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20161)
*Haoxiang Cao,Jiajiong Cao,Xuanpu Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chaoqun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS是一个针对指令式图像编辑中"规划器-渲染器"两阶段流水线的强化学习框架，通过跨模块和跨模块内的双层级信用分配，将结果级奖励转化为局部化监督，在五个基准上优于同等设置下的Joint RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规划器-渲染器的两阶段图像编辑系统中，仅用最终图像奖励训练效率低下，因为糟糕的编辑结果无法区分问题出在规划器还是渲染器，且自由形式的推理轨迹难以定位具体错误来源，导致信用分配困难。

Method: DARS框架包含两个层面的信用分配机制：1) 跨模块层面，通过多规划-多渲染的展开（rollout）估计规划间与规划内的奖励变异性，实现软性模块路由，并以rollout平均奖励作为难度估计构建自适应课程；2) 规划器内部，采用四字段结构化推理输出，实现前缀门控奖励（prefix-gated reward）与token级优势重加权，将结果级反馈转化为局部化监督。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验表明，DARS在相同骨干网络、数据、奖励模型和rollout预算条件下优于Joint RL基线，在推理密集型编辑任务上取得了最大收益。

Conclusion: DARS通过双层级信用分配有效解决了指令式图像编辑中两阶段强化学习的信用分配难题，验证了结构化推理输出与软模块路由在提升训练效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing uses a planner-renderer pipeline: a vision-language model (VLM) first converts the instruction into an edit plan, and a diffusion model then executes that plan. Training such systems with only final-image rewards is inefficient because a poor edit does not reveal whether additional optimization should place more emphasis on the planner or the renderer, and even planner-dominant cases remain difficult to localize within a free-form reasoning trace. We present DARS, a reinforcement learning framework for dual-level credit assignment in this two-stage setting. Across modules, multi-plan multi-render rollouts estimate between-plan and within-plan reward variability for soft module routing, while rollout mean rewards provide hardness estimates for an adaptive curriculum. Within the planner, a four-field structured reasoning output enables a prefix-gated reward and token-level advantage reweighting, turning outcome-level feedback into localized supervision. Experiments on five benchmarks show that DARS outperforms a Joint~RL baseline with the same backbone, data, reward model, and rollout budget, with the largest gains on reasoning-intensive edits.

</details>


### [98] [The Third Restructuring of Software Form: From the Three-Tier Architecture to Storage, Models, and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20201)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出软件范式正在经历第三次转变——Software 3.0，即由上下文与推理决定行为。其终极形态收敛为三个要素：广义数据库（统一持久化抽象）、大模型（智能推理核心）和智能体（连接前两者的执行循环）。传统三层架构中，UI层被模型按需生成界面所吸收，业务逻辑层按"可表达性×关键性"重划分为模型推理与存储约束（残余确定性逻辑保留为工具），仅数据层升格为唯一持久基础设施。论文形式化论证了这一收敛命题，提出最小参考架构，并用真实原型和在线模型提供证据，系统分析了该命题成立的条件与失效边界（确定性、成本、安全与可验证性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件形态先后经历了指令决定行为的Software 1.0和数据决定行为的Software 2.0（机器学习）两次范式转变。作者认为第三次转变——由上下文和推理决定行为的Software 3.0——正在进行中，并试图论证其终极形态将收敛为统一的架构范式，从而系统性地说明软件工程未来的发展方向。

Method: 作者首先形式化地定义了Software 3.0收敛命题，提出由广义数据库、大模型和智能体组成的最小参考架构；随后基于"可表达性×关键性"二维划分对传统三层架构进行重新剖析，论证UI层和业务逻辑层的吸收与重分配；最后通过真实原型和在线模型的实际部署来收集证据，并对命题的成立条件（可表达、可验证、外部有状态、工具完备）和失效边界（确定性、成本、安全、可验证性）进行系统分析。

Result: 论文提出了一个清晰的三阶段范式演进框架（1.0指令、2.0数据、3.0上下文与推理），给出了Software 3.0的最小参考架构，并通过原型和在线模型提供了实证证据。论证表明该命题在可表达、可验证、外部有状态且工具完备的任务域中成立，同时识别了确定性、成本、安全和可验证性四个限制适用范围的边界条件。

Conclusion: 作者认为Software 3.0范式将在特定任务域内成立并重塑开发者角色、数据库产业和软件工程学科，但其适用范围受确定性、成本、安全与可验证性四重边界所限定。这一论断对理解AI时代软件架构的演化方向具有重要的前瞻性意义。

Abstract: Software form has undergone two paradigm shifts since its inception: Software 1.0, in which instructions determine behavior, and Software 2.0, in which data determines behavior (machine learning). This paper argues that a third shift - Software 3.0, in which context and reasoning determine behavior - is now underway, and contends that its terminal form converges to three elements: a generalized database (the unified abstraction of all persistent state and memory), a large model (the intelligence core that performs reasoning and generation), and an agent (the execution loop connecting the first two). The core argument is as follows: in the traditional three-tier architecture, the user-interface layer will be absorbed by the model's ability to generate interfaces on demand, the business-logic layer will be re-partitioned along "expressibility x criticality" into model reasoning and storage constraints (with residual deterministic logic retained as tools), and only the data layer will be elevated into the sole persistent infrastructure. We formalize this convergence thesis, present a minimal reference architecture, report evidence from real prototypes and a live model, and systematically analyze both the conditions under which it holds and the boundaries where it fails - determinism, cost, security, and verifiability delimit the thesis's domain of applicability. We argue that the thesis holds in task domains that are expressible, verifiable, externally stateful, and tool-complete, and that it will reshape the roles of developers, the database industry, and the software-engineering discipline.

</details>


### [99] [MemTrapBench: Benchmarking Cognitive Traps in LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20202)
*Mengru Wang,Haozhe Luo,Zhenqian Xu,Zhixiang Cui,Haoming Xu,Qu Yang,Jizhan Fang,Junfeng Fang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemTrapBench 基准，用于评估大语言模型中记忆对当前任务推理的负面影响（即"认知陷阱"），并提出了 AdaptiveMem 推理时方法来缓解这些问题，使模型在避免记忆陷阱的同时不牺牲标准记忆任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估信息是否正确提取、存储和检索，却忽略了检索到的记忆如何重塑模型推理并影响当前任务表现。作者识别出记忆诱导的认知陷阱：即使记忆被忠实记录且在语义上相关，仍可能扭曲模型的推理或信念，降低当前任务性能。

Method: 构建 MemTrapBench 基准，涵盖两类认知陷阱：推理固定（Reasoning Fixation）和信念扭曲（Belief Distortion）。提出 AdaptiveMem，一种简单有效的推理时方法，通过指示 LLM 避开记忆陷阱来缓解认知陷阱。

Result: 在两类模型家族和五种代表性记忆框架上的实验显示，所有评估的记忆策略在 MemTrapBench 上均低于无记忆设置，最强方法也遭受超过10%的性能下降。AdaptiveMem 能够缓解 MemTrapBench 上的认知陷阱，同时在各种记忆框架上保持或提升标准记忆基准的性能。

Conclusion: 记忆并非总是有益的，即使检索到正确且相关的记忆也可能对当前任务产生负面影响。MemTrapBench 为评估记忆系统的认知陷阱提供了系统化工具，AdaptiveMem 为缓解此类问题提供了简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory has become a key component of large language models, enabling them to retain information and learn from long-term interactions. However, existing memory benchmarks mainly evaluate whether information is correctly extracted, stored, and retrieved, while largely overlooking how retrieved memories reshape model reasoning and affect performance on the current task. We identify memory-induced cognitive traps: even faithfully recorded and semantically relevant memories can distort model reasoning or beliefs and degrade current task performance. To systematically evaluate these failure modes, we introduce MemTrapBench, which covers two forms of cognitive traps: Reasoning Fixation and Belief Distortion. Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [100] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContractScrub，这是首个用于评估LLM合同审查（contract scrubbing）能力的基准测试，包含由资深律师手工构建的涵盖多种错误类别的合同样本。实验发现前沿模型在此任务上表现意外地差，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管它们在相关通用基准上表现优异，揭示了当前模型的实际局限以及构建领域特定基准的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同审查是法律工作中高度依赖LLM的典型场景，具有自动化潜力（例行、细致、需长上下文推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别），但此前缺乏对LLM在此任务上能力的正式评估。

Method: 构建ContractScrub基准：由经验丰富的律师手工制作合同样本，覆盖多种错误类别（如定义术语误用、错误引用、语言不一致），用于系统评估前沿LLM的合同审查能力。

Result: 前沿模型在ContractScrub上表现不佳，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管它们在看似相关的通用基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 当前模型在合同审查这一具有重要经济价值的任务上存在实际能力局限，凸显了针对特定领域、精准设计的基准对于衡量模型真实世界影响的重要性。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [101] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的方法来自动分类电子海图（ENC）变更的关键性风险，通过设计包含空间上下文编码器和ENC属性编码器的基线编码方案，将复杂的矢量数据变更转化为结构化表格格式，在两个运营数据集上使用梯度提升树实现了90%和94%的准确率，相比默认模型提升5-7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文办公室面临的核心挑战在于判断海图变更是构成关键还是非关键的海上安全风险。现有工作流高度依赖人工审查和验证，劳动密集、难以随海图更新量扩展，且不同分析人员之间存在不一致性。因此需要一种自动化方法来解决这一瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出一个用于ENC变更自动分类的方法。建立基线编码方案，将复杂的矢量数据变更翻译为结构化表格格式以供分类模型使用。编码方案包含两个关键组件：(1)空间上下文编码器，用于丰富变更表示中的周围地理特征；(2)ENC属性编码器，用于表示修改对象细微的属性值描述。使用调优的梯度提升树作为分类模型。

Result: 在两个不同的运营数据集（包含1,308个海图对和超过100,000个单独海图修改）上评估，调优的梯度提升树分别达到90%和94%的准确率，相比使用默认超参数且不含空间上下文和属性嵌入的模型提升5-7%。实验还验证了简单位置和空间聚合方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明将机器学习集成到运营地理空间管线中是可行的，能够改进ENC维护并增强海上安全。同时为未来评估更复杂的空间表示学习技术奠定了基线基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [102] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InsufficiencyBench，首个针对法律AI查询侧信息不足问题的基准测试，涵盖8种缺失要素类别和3种结构失效模式，包含202个基准条目。测试10个前沿模型发现，最高F2仅为0.46，中位召回率0.44，模型要么无差别规避要么在虚构假设下作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准测试假设查询是完全完整的，但实际应用中用户常省略关键法律事实，这些事实可能实质性地决定法律结果。作者希望建立首个专门评估模型是否能识别查询中法律关键信息缺失的基准。

Method: 形式化定义了8种规范缺失要素类别，归纳为3种结构失效模式（switch、gating和fatal prerequisite），构建了202个基准条目（58个基础查询、144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，由执业律师标注。评估10个前沿模型。

Result: 在缺失要素识别任务上，没有任何模型F2分数超过0.46，中位召回率为0.44。模型要么无差别地回避问题，要么在虚构假设下静默作答，没有模型能同时做到：对缺陷查询进行识别和限定回答，同时直接回答完整查询。

Conclusion: 当前前沿法律AI系统在识别查询侧信息不足方面存在显著缺陷，无法区分需要补充信息的查询和可以直接回答的查询，这暴露了法律AI在实际应用中潜在的重大风险。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [103] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleMaze基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在显式及未见规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力，并引入语言-逻辑-函数混合（LLFH）方法自动生成规则，以及解耦多模态规划（DMP）方法将感知、执行与规则验证分离，实验证明DMP显著提升规则遵循与规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs虽然结合了语言推理与视觉感知，但它们在显式或未见规则约束下进行视觉空间规划的能力尚未被充分探索。该场景要求模型同时理解空间布局、解析自然语言规则并规划有效动作，现有研究缺乏对此能力的系统性评估与方法。

Method: 提出了两个核心方法：（1）RuleMaze基准，一个可控的迷宫导航任务，要求MLLMs在遵循不同复杂度的自然语言规则下导航；（2）Language-Logic-Function Hybridization (LLFH)，自动生成自然语言规则并将其翻译为逻辑表示与可执行验证器，消除人工规则工程；（3）Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP)，通过可解释的推理原语将感知、执行与规则验证解耦，实现向更复杂和未见规则的系统性泛化。

Result: 实验表明，与端到端文本规划基线相比，DMP在规则遵循和规划成功率上均取得显著提升，同时提供透明的中间规划轨迹。

Conclusion: RuleMaze为研究MLLMs中基于规则的可解释空间规划建立了原则性基准，DMP通过组件解耦实现了更好的规则泛化能力，为多模态智能体的规划研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [104] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体中技能诱导方式如何影响跨任务迁移进行了全面受控研究，发现子任务级技能诱导优于任务级（后者甚至低于无记忆基线），文本格式技能优于代码格式。作者提出技能效用分数（综合技能特异性和抽象性的联合效应）来预判技能记忆的迁移可靠性，该分数仅需技能和任务描述即可计算，无需执行任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可以从已完成任务中诱导技能并在后续复用，但实际中技能迁移不可靠甚至有害。此前对于"何时技能迁移可靠"这一问题缺乏系统性回答，因此需要厘清技能诱导方式如何影响其跨任务迁移表现。

Method: 开展大规模受控实验，沿两个维度对比技能诱导方法：(1) 任务级 vs 子任务级技能诱导；(2) 文本格式 vs 代码格式技能。进一步提出两个互补的技能属性——特定性（技能与真实任务的匹配紧密程度）和抽象性（技能相关性跨任务的分布均匀程度），并将二者结合为技能效用分数作为预测指标。

Result: 任务级技能大多使智能体性能低于无记忆基线，而子任务级技能平均而言高于基线；文本技能迁移优于代码技能。单独的特异性或抽象性均不能预测任务成功率，但二者的联合效应（技能效用分数）与任务成功一致相关，且子任务级和文本格式技能得分更高。

Conclusion: 技能效用分数可作为技能记忆的实用诊断工具，因为其计算仅需要技能与任务描述（无需任务执行），能在任何新任务运行前预判技能迁移的可靠性，为构建更可靠的LLM智能体技能记忆提供理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [105] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种让推理语言模型自适应分配思考资源的方案：模型在响应首token中选择NoThink、Short或Long三种推理模式之一，该选择通过GRPO在塑造奖励和每种模式独立token上限的约束下联合学习，无需单独的路由器。在1.5B蒸馏模型上，三种模式成功涌现且未见坍缩，在MATH500上保持了相近的准确率（0.782 vs 0.796），同时将平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811个token（减少41%），并能在不重训的情况下迁移到其他基准，在更简单的问题上节省更多token（如GSM8K节省76%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型在强化学习训练下通常在固定的token预算下运作，而非显式自适应的预算分配，这导致在简单问题上过度计算、在困难问题上计算不足。作者希望研究模型能否自主学会分配推理努力程度。

Method: 模型在响应的第一个token处从三种模式中选择：NoThink（快速作答）、Short（简短推理）或Long（扩展推理）。该选择嵌入GRPO框架内联合学习，不依赖单独的路由器模块。通过塑造奖励使每种模式在不同响应长度下都有价值，同时施加每种模式独立的硬性token上限以保持各模式特征分明。

Result: 在1.5B蒸馏模型训练于MATH的实验中，三种模式均涌现且未坍缩为单一选择；简短模式反而比Long模式更准确，说明路由器按难度而非随机分类。三个种子平均后，策略在MATH500上准确率接近基线（0.782 vs 0.796），平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811个token（减少41%）。模型无需重训即可迁移到其他基准，在更简单的问题上节省最多，如GSM8K上减少76%的token且准确率高于同等响应长度的基线。

Conclusion: 作者构建了一个能够自适应选择每道问题推理量的推理模型，在几乎不损失准确率的情况下大幅削减计算开销，并且具有良好的跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [106] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3-8B模型的三轮LoRA自训练进行了审计，发现七个测量伪影，每个都会在缺少对照时颠倒报告结论。作者提出一种基于FDR控制的按问题精确检验方法，替代有缺陷的扩展统计量。审计发现外部蒸馏能改进基础模型很少覆盖的问题，而三种自训练形式不能，且自训练会在基础水平上损坏已解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型是否自我改进的判断标准正从平均准确率转向个体问题的得失，但这需要对两个噪声估计做差分，容易受测量伪影影响。现有评估方法缺乏严格的对照和错误率控制，导致结论可能被测量伪影倒置。作者旨在建立过渡级（transition-level）审计方法，要求每个统计量都有单独测量的零假设。

Method: 对rank-32 LoRA自训练的三轮实验进行审计，使用冻结的对照组通过完全相同的pipeline推送。发现七类测量失败后，作者提出用基于池化基线的逐问题精确检验替代自然阈值修复，并在FDR控制下进行检验。该检验在保留复现中不检测任何结果，且对多重检验规则、错误率和池大小不敏感。随后在匹配流、量和评估的阶梯式实验臂上进行应用审计。

Result: 单次贪婪解码构建的账本能在未训练模型上制造能力变化，主要是推理批处理的伪影；扩展统计量给该模型分配0.280的获取率。自然阈值修复无法通过复现，其零假设非零。新的精确检验在所有保留复现上不检测到任何结果。审计发现外部蒸馏改进基础模型很少触及的问题而三种自训练不能，回归检验拒绝该不对称性仅为蒸馏总体增益更大的副产品（p<10^-8）。自训练在基础水平上损坏已解决问题的速率远超测量下限。

Conclusion: 过渡级审计要求每个报告的统计量都配有一个单独测量的零假设。这些零假设不需要新实验，可以从多臂研究已有的基础水平复现中构建，但前提是复现数量足够，仅少数几个复现是不够的。换句话说，稳健的模型改进评估必须内置对照组和足够的复现，否则结论极易被测量伪影反转。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [107] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次在Solana生态系统中进行大规模rug pull早期检测，构建了包含640万代币、跨越7个月的数据集。研究发现大多数memecoin在发布一小时内就表现出rug pull特征，且仅使用前5分钟交易数据的XGBoost等经典机器学习模型即可实现稳健检测；此外，PumpFun和Raydium之间的跨平台数据融合可有效缓解域偏移，提升检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着memecoin在区块链平台上的快速激增，rug pull等欺诈活动的风险显著增加。以往研究主要聚焦于Ethereum上的代币，但Solana已成为memecoin交易量和代币数量的领先区块链。与Ethereum依赖智能合约后门不同，Solana上的rug pull主要由流动性操纵和社交动态驱动，因此需要专门针对该生态的早期检测研究。

Method: 构建了涵盖640万代币、跨越7个月的Solana memecoin数据集。采用经典机器学习模型（重点是XGBoost梯度提升模型），仅使用代币发布后前5分钟的交易数据特征进行rug pull检测。同时评估了PumpFun和Raydium两个平台之间的跨平台泛化能力，并采用多源数据融合策略来缓解域偏移问题。

Result: 市场分析表明绝大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出rug pull特征，凸显了短时预测的紧迫性。XGBoost模型仅凭前5分钟交易数据即实现了稳健的rug pull检测性能。跨平台评估显示多源数据融合显著缓解了域偏移，提高了检测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为保护投资者提供了实用框架，填补了Solana生态系统中rug pull早期检测的研究空白。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [108] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4AI-Bench，一个包含10个冻结研究仓库、覆盖10种训练算法家族的基准测试，用于隔离评估AI系统设计训练算法的能力（即递归自我改进的关键能力）。基准采用统一的0到1评分尺度，将每个任务的算法改进效果标准化。实验显示：在29种系统配置下，平均得分仅为0.166，最强系统也只有0.250——即使最强系统也只关闭了原始算法与最优解之间不到五分之一的距离。大多数提交根本没有改变模型学习方式，而真正改变学习方式的提交平均得分0.226 vs 0.126。更多推理投入能将真正修改算法提交比例从8%提升到64%，平均分从0.094提升到0.196。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的核心问题在于AI系统能否改进产生AI系统的训练算法本身。然而现有基准测试要么被数据收集或超参数调优所主导，要么无法区分"执行的改变"与"模型学习的改变"。因此需要一个专门隔离测试"智能体设计训练算法能力"的基准，以判定RSI的可行性。

Method: 构建AI4AI-Bench：选取10个冻结的研究仓库，覆盖10个训练算法家族。每个任务中，智能体在B300上4小时内重写训练算法，随后其代码从零重新运行最长12小时，由对智能体隐藏的固定评估器打分，并与同一程序下仓库原始算法比较。由于10个任务的指标不可通约，将每个任务映射到统一尺度（0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库原始算法，1.0为任务最优）。

Result: 在6个系统的29种配置下，所有10个任务的平均得分为0.166，最强系统达0.250——最强系统仅闭合了原始算法与最优解之间不到五分之一的距离。大多数提交从未改变模型的学习方式；少数真正改变学习方式的提交均分0.226 vs 未改变的0.126。增加推理努力使真正修改学习方式的提交从8%上升到64%，均分从0.094升到0.196。

Conclusion: 当前最强的AI系统在改进训练算法这一RSI关键能力上表现非常有限，大多数系统甚至不会去修改模型的学习方式。RSI尚不可行，但增加推理努力显著提升了系统尝试修改训练算法的意愿和效果，表明该能力可能随系统能力提升而改善。作者开源了整个任务套件、评估器和所有得分提交，以便随系统演进重复测量。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [109] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个三智能体工作流，整合对话式数据采集、结构化数据处理与行为预测。通过聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集了454条学生通勤者出行模式选择数据，评估了多项Logit模型、随机森林及9个本地部署的大型语言模型（2-35B参数）。最佳视觉配置达到71.5%五分类准确率，随机森林与最优文本零样本LLM分别达到69.6%和69.9%，展示了多智能体工作流在可审计研究流程中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究越来越多地将数字数据采集与预测建模相结合，但这两个阶段通常被分开开发和评估，缺乏统一协调的工作流程。

Method: 提出三智能体工作流：（1）聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查采集数据；（2）结构化数据处理与多项Logit模型分析；（3）九个本地部署LLM（2-35B参数）在四种零样本提示条件下及persona、few-shot、视觉增强配置下的行为预测，并与随机森林和逻辑回归进行基准对比。

Result: 随机森林达到69.6%五分类准确率；最佳文本零样本LLM达到69.9%无需任务特定拟合；few-shot prompting提升多个模型性能且增益在小样本后趋于稳定；使用相同天气图像的最佳视觉配置达到71.5%准确率；习惯性出行信息带来最一致提升，Expert框架整体优于Role-Play。

Conclusion: 对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习与多模态LLM预测可以在可审计的多智能体工作流中协调运作，视觉上下文可为特定模型提供额外预测信息。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


### [110] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool，一个面向通用工具使用能力的开放语料构建管线，通过整合网络、PDF、代码数据与真实工具API、MCP技能及文档型工作流的合成监督信号，对Qwen3-4B/8B-Base进行中期训练，在SFT和RL后训练条件下均显著提升BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe上的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中期训练已被证明能增强数学、科学等推理密集型能力及软件工程智能体能力，但通用工具使用这一同等重要的智能体能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出MidTool开放语料构建管线，融合大规模网络、PDF和代码数据，并利用真实工具API、MCP技能和文档型工作流生成合成监督信号。课程设计教授模型识别工具可用性、从上下文中提取参数、组合工具调用工作流以及从信息不完整中恢复。在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中期训练，随后进行SFT和RL后训练。

Result: 与基线相比，MidTool-Mix在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe三个基准上，在SFT和RL两种后训练设置下均持续提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用能力与其他重要LLM能力一样，应从专门的中期训练中获益，而非完全依赖后训练阶段来习得。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [111] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pandora's Router和Pandora's Bidder两种新策略，将异构AI系统中的路由问题形式化为Pandora's Box（带代价检查的最优搜索问题），通过价值信息（value-of-information）计算决定是否值得为每个专家细化价值估计，在集中式和去中心化场景下均能显著减少昂贵的估计器调用次数，同时保持路由质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统中将查询路由给最合适的专家模型可以提升质量与效率，但估计每个专家的预期回报本身有代价：廉价估计器快但噪声大，昂贵估计器准但开销高。这种代价-精度权衡需要被形式化地建模和优化，而非依赖启发式方法。

Method: 将路由中"是否进一步细化价值估计"的决策形式化为Pandora's Box问题。在高斯信号模型假设下，得到闭式表达的价值信息公式，决定每个专家和输入是否需要花费成本细化估计。集中式策略为Pandora's Router；去中心化扩展为Pandora's Bidder，允许专家独立决定是否投资自我评估后再接受报价认领查询。

Result: 在三个领域（多LLM基准、检索增强专家、可变推理时长的LLM）的实验中，Pandora's Router在保持与穷举估计相同的路由质量的同时，大幅减少了昂贵估计器的调用次数。去中心化场景下，价值信息推理在竞争估计准确时提升分配效率；但当竞争估计噪声大时，可能增加策略性专家的效用而损害他人。

Conclusion: 基于Pandora's Box框架的代价-感知路由策略能有效平衡估计成本与路由质量，为异构AI系统的有效路由提供了理论依据和实用算法；同时揭示了去中心化场景下信息价值推理的潜在博弈和激励问题。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications的专家排名与基于7,714个真实LLM安全事件（来自CVE、GHSA、OSV、AIAAIC）的实际数据排名进行对比，发现两者一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20），但专家排名依然稳健。作者提出以0.75专家权重+0.25数据权重的混合2026候选列表，并指出预注册的四个前沿分类器在基准测试中均无法超越简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10是安全从业者社区基于专家经验投票产生的风险排名，但该排名是否与实际发生的LLM安全事故数据吻合从未被系统性验证。作者希望用真实事件数据检验专家排名，回答"专家共识是否与数据一致"这一核心问题。

Method: 从CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC四个来源收集7,714个LLM安全事件快照，其中6,639个按20类分类体系标注；使用贝叶斯测量误差模型对每个类别的计数进行精确率和召回率校正，从而得出基于事件的排名；将专家投票与数据排名以0.75:0.25的固定权重融合形成2026候选列表；通过预注册的四分类器基准测试（bake-off）验证分类器性能，并与基于发生率（incidence floor）的简单基线进行对比。

Result: 专家排名与数据排名的一致性很弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%置信区间跨越零）；四类前沿分类器在预注册基准测试中均未胜出，且无一超过发生率基线的平衡准确率0.863；基于发生率顺序的基线在留出真实数据上仍保持较高排序一致性（Spearman ρ=0.918）。

Conclusion: 尽管专家排名与实际事件数据的相关性较弱，专家排名仍具鲁棒性。当前无分类器能在事件分类中超越简单基线，发生率方法作为下界排序依然有效。该研究为探索性分析，由工作组两位成员完成，不替代OWASP官方列表或流程。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>


### [113] [ABEAT: Efficient and Anonymous Encryption for ABE-based Dynamic Group Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19302)
*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABEAT，一个基于属性基加密（ABE）的匿名动态群组通信系统。ABEAT通过图命名空间实现群组的动态形成与变化，并引入新的匿名KP-ABE方法来隐藏密文中的属性，从而维护接收者匿名性，同时大幅降低非目标接收者的解密开销（比现有最优方法HVE快90倍以上，比不具备匿名性的FABEO还低40%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现动态群组参与者之间的机密、灵活且高效的多对多通信是重要需求，但现有基于ABE的群组通信方案在维护接收者匿名性方面存在不足，尤其针对应急响应等多样化场景，需要在保证保密通信的同时隐藏消息接收者身份，且不能给非目标接收者带来过高解密开销。

Method: ABEAT结合属性基加密与图命名空间构建动态群组通信框架，并提出一种新的匿名KP-ABE方法：在KP-ABE密文中隐藏明文属性，以防止多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击。系统通过快速接收者验证机制，使非目标接收者能迅速判定自身不在接收者集合内，避免不必要的完整解密运算。

Result: ABEAT在保持接收者匿名性的同时，将非接收者的解密开销相比当前最优技术HVE降低了90倍以上，甚至比不提供匿名性的FABEO方案还要低40%，实现了安全性与效率的兼顾。

Conclusion: ABEAT证明了在动态群组通信中同时实现高效性和接收者匿名性是可行的，其匿名KP-ABE方法有效抵御了针对匿名性的攻击，且显著优于现有方案，为应急响应等对匿名性和效率均有高要求的应用场景提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>


### [114] [Aray: Deterministic-First Synthesis of Benign Artifacts for YARA Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19387)
*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA规则解释器与正向样本合成器，可在不重建原始恶意样本的前提下，为YARA规则生成等价匹配的良性测试样本（fixture）。在416条公开规则评估中，406条（97.6%）成功构造并通过原始规则校验，且无扫描器不匹配或构造失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但能验证其匹配的恶意样本难以共享，给存储、持续集成、灾难恢复演练和可复现的扫描器验证带来困难。简单嵌入字面量无法满足要求，因为YARA条件可组合备选、计数、偏移、整数读取和可执行容器约束。正向验证是存在性问题——只需要规则匹配集中的任意一个文件级成员，而非重建原始样本。

Method: Aray采用确定性优先的架构：模型可提出构造性规范化或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不接触后端源码或二进制结构。常规代码负责验证规范化规则、推导字符串和整数证人（witness）、执行提取、路由、碰撞检查布局以及ELF/PE/通用序列化。仅残余的规范化语义交由有界模型评判。该流程还包括构建前检查（preflight）和原始规则oracle来验证生成样本对源规则的匹配。

Result: 在416条公开规则条目上评估：无模型辅助下规范化接受182条，模型规范化后接受234条，共416条全部完成规范化。构建前检查准入406条，所有准入样本均匹配其上游原始规则。整体成功率406/416（97.6%），可构造规则中406/406（100%）。有10条预期的preflight处置，无扫描器不匹配或构造失败。一个不可达的端点确认了实现阶段零模型调用。两个锚定正则失败在最终运行前已修复，因此结果是修复后系统结果而非留出集估计。

Conclusion: Aray有效解决了YARA规则正向匹配样本的合成问题，将存在性验证与规则重建解耦。原始规则oracle可验证生成样本对源规则的匹配；证明对所有可能文件的蕴含关系仍是另一个更强的独立目标。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>


### [115] [Redactable blockchains and polynomial equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19401)
*Alexander Demin,Alexey Ovchinnikov,Vladimir Shpilrain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文开发了新的工具，用于构建具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构。其核心创新在于构造的单项函数求逆等价于求解多元多项式方程组，而该问题目前被认为是量子安全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，现有的基于传统密码学假设（如大整数分解、离散对数）的认证数据结构将面临安全威胁。需要开发基于后量子安全假设的可编辑认证数据结构（redactable authenticated data structures），以满足量子时代的数据完整性和隐私保护需求。

Method: 提出了一种新的构造方法，其核心是设计一种新的单向函数，该函数的求逆问题等价于求解一个（或多个）多元多项式方程。由于多元多项式方程组求解问题目前没有已知的高效量子算法，因此该构造具有后量子安全性。

Result: 成功构建了具有后量子安全性的可编辑认证数据结构，其安全性基于求解多元多项式方程（或方程组）的计算困难性假设，该假设在当前已知量子算法下是安全的（在参数选择合理的前提下）。

Conclusion: 本文为后量子安全的可编辑认证数据结构提供了新的构造工具，其安全性建立在多元多项式方程求解困难性这一量子安全假设之上，为量子计算时代的数据认证和可编辑性需求提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>


### [116] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个实用的联邦学习框架，使用Tailscale提供安全网络通信、Flower框架进行服务器端聚合，支持地理分布式协作开发口腔癌筛查的稳健AI模型。通过FedAvg聚合轻量级模型，MobileNetV4-Conv-Small表现最佳，AUC达0.929，准确率87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域数据获取受法规和患者隐私限制，尤其是临床口腔图像难以获得。联邦学习能够在不集中或共享患者数据的前提下，利用分散数据集进行协作模型开发，解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 构建了一个实用的FL框架：客户端设备通过Tailscale实现安全网络连接和实时通信；使用Flower框架进行服务器端聚合；客户端部署和编排采用手动配置，不依赖企业级FL平台。评估了MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large和MobileNetV4-Conv-Small三种轻量级移动端友好架构，并使用FedAvg进行全局模型聚合。

Result: 在FedAvg聚合的全局轻量级模型中，基于MobileNetV4-Conv-Small的全局模型（GM-V4）表现最佳，AUC达到0.929，准确率为87%。

Conclusion: 该联邦学习框架证明可在不依赖企业FL平台的情况下，实现跨地理分布的协作式医疗AI模型开发，且轻量级架构能够支撑智能手机端口腔癌筛查应用。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [117] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
