*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS（注意力-嵌入梯度隔离盾），一种轻量级防御方法，通过三项反向传播操作同时关闭Transformer梯度中泄露私有文本信息的三个分析通道（注意力输出投影、嵌入梯度、MLP扩展梯度），在不改变架构的情况下将梯度反转攻击的token恢复率降至接近零，同时保持甚至提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度反转攻击可从头共享梯度中恢复私有训练文本，构成了严重的隐私威胁。现有攻击方法已通过对Transformer梯度结构的分析实现了近乎精确的token恢复，而现有防御方案最多只处理一个泄露通道，要么降低模型效用，要么留下其他结构信号。亟需一种能够同时关闭所有分析泄露通道的轻量级防御方法。

Method: AEGIS通过三项反向传播路径操作实现防御：(1) 冻结注意力投影参数，从构造上消除通道1（注意力输出投影梯度中的低秩子空间泄露）；(2) 向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声，破坏通道2的token存在性信号；(3) 向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块等效噪声，掩盖通道3。同一掩码梯度同时驱动本地优化器更新和服务器导出，确保两侧均无干净信号残留。对通道1和2提供形式化保证，并针对完全了解机制的自适应攻击者进行了经验验证。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估表明，AEGIS将token恢复率降至接近零，能够抵御包括分析型和基于优化的各类梯度反转攻击，同时保持或提升模型效用。

Conclusion: AEGIS是首个能够同时关闭Transformer梯度中所有三个分析泄露通道的轻量级防御方案，无需架构变更，在隐私保护与模型效用之间取得了良好平衡，并对自适应攻击者具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [118] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种双混淆方法，通过结合随机二元权重的鲁棒性增强技术和图像加密技术，联合混淆梯度更新信息和训练图像，以增强联邦学习对图像重建攻击（如APRIL）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中虽然数据保存在本地，但已有研究表明共享的模型更新可以重建训练数据，存在严重的隐私泄露风险，亟需有效的防御方法。

Method: 提出双重混淆方法：(1) 基于随机二元权重的鲁棒性增强技术，随机将部分梯度元素置零，减少攻击者可利用的原始梯度信息；(2) 图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。两者互补保护，且图像加密支持每客户端和每图像独立密钥，避免显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer (ViT)的图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且除图像加密本身的性能损失外，未带来额外的分类性能下降。

Conclusion: 虽然组合方法不能提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果证明了梯度修改与图像加密相结合在隐私增强联邦学习中的潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [119] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点的自适应APT攻击调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空指纹对齐三个组件，在DARPA E3数据集上分别达到95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS降低了约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击的多阶段隐蔽特性使传统检测方法失效，虽然溯源图能提供丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且淹没在大量常规系统活动中，导致全图学习计算开销巨大且难以关联长序列攻击。

Method: TGL-APT包含三个互补组件：(1)信息瓶颈引导的图蒸馏，抑制溯源冗余同时约束结构失真并保持因果可达性；(2)自适应时序图学习，随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3)跨时空攻击指纹对齐，关联不同实体和时间窗口中的碎片化可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段刻画重构连贯的攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上F1分数分别为95.7%、90.9%和88.9%，相比KAIROS降低了约39%的训练时间、33%的检测延迟和22%的内存使用。

Conclusion: TGL-APT有效平衡了检测性能、计算效率和调查能力，为基于溯源图的APT分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [120] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将勒索软件执行建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，采用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密过程，所生成的攻击模式能够逃避行为监控器的指纹检测，揭示了基于搜索的方法在生成规避性恶意软件方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件的大规模加密操作会立即触发现代防御系统的检测。现代威胁需要保持数小时而非数分钟的隐蔽性，以弥合持久性差距（persistence gap）。作者旨在解决传统勒索软件容易被立即识别的根本问题，探索一种范式转变，将攻击执行转化为一个可优化的搜索问题。

Method: 将勒索软件执行形式化为SBSE优化问题，使用遗传算法（GA）对数据加密过程进行优化。优化目标是在系统活动统计偏差的硬性约束下最大化加密效率/效果，使攻击行为在统计上与基线系统活动保持不可区分，从而规避行为监控器的指纹检测。

Result: 实验证明所演化的攻击模式能够成功规避行为监控器（behavioral monitors）的指纹检测技术，即在保持加密有效性的同时，系统活动的统计特征与正常基线无明显偏差。

Conclusion: 基于搜索的方法为生成逃避性恶意软件提供了强有力的框架，这为自动化软件防御界提出了新的挑战，暗示防御系统需要发展更鲁棒的检测策略来应对这类由演化算法驱动的自适应攻击。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [121] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MaliciousSkillBench，一个综合的恶意Agent Skill检测基准，整合13个公开数据源并将8,414条原始恶意记录归一化为7,539个唯一身份（4,588个结构家族），最终构建包含9,740个Skill（7,505恶意、2,235良性）的主基准。评测发现，现有检测器在跨源场景下性能显著下降，指出可靠检测需要更广的跨源覆盖和联合衡量攻击检测与良性误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills通过可复用的指令包扩展LLM Agent能力（可包含脚本、资源和服务配置），这为恶意行为创造了直接分发渠道。然而，现有恶意Skill数据集分散在不同来源、工件格式、证据制度和良性覆盖范围中，且存在重复和结构相关的内容，使得直接聚合和评测变得复杂。作者旨在构建一个全面的基准来解决这一碎片化问题。

Method: 作者整合13个公开数据源（其中11个贡献核心恶意工件），通过归一化去重将8,414条原始恶意记录缩减为7,539个唯一身份和4,588个操作结构家族；进行保守的跨标签冲突排除后构建了包含9,740个Skill（7,505恶意、2,235良性）的主基准。同时整理了11种攻击类别以覆盖4,983个恶意身份，并评估了三种学习型文本检测器和三种现成Skill扫描器，包括Random、structural-disjoint和Source-Disjoint三种评测设置。

Result: 学习型检测器在Random设置下达到0.882-0.932的Random Macro-F1，但在Source-Disjoint评测下仅0.653-0.665；最优的word TF-IDF SVM在Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint上分别得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保留95.6%恶意召回但产生62.4%的良性误报率。现成扫描器处于不同但不令人满意的操作区间，降低误报的同时大幅牺牲恶意召回。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可靠的恶意Skill检测需要更广泛的跨源基准覆盖，以及能够联合衡量攻击检测能力和良性过报（over-flagging）的评测方式。现有方法在不同评测设置下均未达到令人满意的权衡，凸显了跨源泛化和低误报高召回检测器的必要性。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [122] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个面向机密计算环境的POSIX兼容文件系统，通过简洁加密承诺和鉴权数据结构，在无需修改应用程序的前提下为持久化存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保障，抵御云服务商的回滚、重放、分叉和篡改攻击。基于ZFS的实现ShieldZFS在提供强安全保证的同时性能与现有先进文件系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算通过TEE保护计算过程，但存储仍然暴露在恶意云服务商面前。即使有磁盘加密，攻击者仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜性保证。现有方案要么假设存储可信、要么开销过高，要么需要应用层介入，缺乏一个无需修改应用且开销可控的通用解决方案。

Method: ShieldFS采用简洁密码学承诺表示合法的文件系统状态，承诺保存在TEE内部并在轻量级可信注册表中复制。盘上数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）使用哈希链和内嵌Merkle树进行鉴权。通过事务和写时复制（copy-on-write）机制原子地更新持久化文件系统状态和承诺，并在读取时验证承诺以检测回滚、重放和歧义攻击。实现上通过扩展ZFS得到ShieldZFS。

Result: 基于标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载的评估表明，ShieldZFS在提供强完整性和新鲜性保证的同时，性能与当前最先进的文件系统（state-of-the-art）相当。该设计即使在整个I/O栈不可信的情况下也能有效检测各类攻击。

Conclusion: ShieldFS证明了在不修改应用程序的前提下，为机密计算环境中的持久化存储提供端到端完整性和新鲜性保障是可行的，且性能开销可接受。这填补了机密计算在存储安全方面的空白，为有状态应用提供了完整的安全保障。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [123] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath 将可验证凭证的撤销状态查询从验证者转移至持有者，通过零知识证明使持有者证明凭证未在验证者选定的注册表根下被撤销，从而消除验证者可见的状态元数据，防止跨会话的凭证关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证凭证在每次出示时无需签发者参与，但撤销机制会引入隐私问题。现有撤销状态检查可能暴露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可被用作稳定句柄来关联不同次的凭证出示，破坏持有者的隐私模型。

Method: ShadowPath 将凭证状态查找移至持有者端。每次出示时，持有者以零知识方式证明凭证未在验证者选定的注册表根下被撤销，验证者仅获知状态结果而不接触可观察元数据。论文首次评估了 Verkle 树用于凭证撤销的可行性，并与稀疏梅克尔树（SMT）进行比较，测试 Groth16 证明系统下的性能差异。

Result: 在 30 次桌面实验中，稀疏梅克尔树的中位 Groth16 证明时间为 371.6ms，Verkle 树为 2.11s；验证时间分别为 3.70ms 和 7.55ms。两台主流移动设备上 Groth16 Verkle 证明耗时约 3 秒。

Conclusion: 更短的身份验证路径并不必然带来更廉价的零知识证明。在会话随机值独立性假设下，验证者可见的状态数据不会揭示两次出示是否使用同一凭证，但该保证不涵盖签发者-验证者串通及同步流量场景。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [124] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于指令微调LLM的标准化分类框架用于异常认证行为日志检测。在受控网络安全测试床上构建包含正常、边界和异常场景的端点专属数据集，评估了Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct和GPT-OSS 20B三个模型，并与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch统计异常检测进行对比。Meta Llama表现最佳（准确率89.3%，F1 91.8%，边界场景检出率80%），显著优于Wazuh（准确率52%，边界检出率20%）和OpenSearch（准确率49.3%，边界检出率15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常行为日志检测方法存在明显不足：基于规则的检测（如Wazuh）依赖预定义规则，难以处理未知或边界情况；统计异常检测（如OpenSearch）只能识别与历史模式偏差，缺乏语义理解。虽然LLM已被探索用于日志异常检测，但存在提示构建不稳定、日志噪声干扰以及对通用数据集的依赖等问题，缺乏端点专属的认证行为数据。这些局限性促使本研究开发更鲁棒的基于LLM的分类框架。

Method: 1) 构建受控网络安全测试床，生成端点专属认证数据，制作涵盖正常、边界和异常三类行为场景的精选数据集；2) 开发标准化指令式LLM分类框架用于检测异常认证行为（含边界案例）；3) 评估三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）；4) 与Wazuh规则检测和OpenSearch异常检测进行对比，使用统一的地面真值严重性框架进行公平评估。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得最佳端到端检测性能：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1分数91.8%、假阴性率11.8%，且检测出80%的边界异常场景。相比之下，Wazuh准确率仅52.0%、假阴性率68.6%、边界检出率20%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴性率74.5%、边界检出率15%。Qwen推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效率达100%，GPT-OSS在产生有效响应时分类性能强劲。

Conclusion: 基于指令微调LLM的分类框架在异常认证行为日志检测方面显著优于传统规则检测和统计异常检测方法，尤其在边界场景检测上优势明显。开源模型Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct在综合检测性能上表现最优，Qwen凭借低延迟和高响应可靠性适合延迟敏感场景。这证明了LLM语义理解能力在网络安全日志分析中的实用价值，为端点专属异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [125] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究对比了82名IT专业人员对三款分别于2019、2022和2024年发布的语音合成工具生成音频的检测能力，并将其与六个预训练的深度伪造检测器进行基准比较。结果显示，对于完全合成的语音，ElevenLabs生成的音频（F1=48%）比RTVC和YourTTS（F1≈90%）更难被人类识别；对于部分伪造场景，严格准确率骤降至9%，且人类和检测器都难以可靠定位短时篡改，同时人类还会越来越多地将真实语音误判为伪造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于语音合成技术的快速进步导致deepfake音频高度逼真，而此前人类检测准确率（70-80%）的结论主要基于较旧的声音合成器，缺乏对现代合成工具下人类检测能力的系统评估。

Method: 招募82名IT专业人员，使用三款不同年份（2019、2022、2024）发布的语音合成工具（RTVC、YourTTS、ElevenLabs）生成测试材料，分别测试完全伪造（full spoof）和部分伪造（partial spoof）两种场景下的人类检测表现，同时用六个预训练检测器在同一材料上进行基准对比。

Result: 完全合成语音的F1分数从RTVC/YourTTS的约90%降至ElevenLabs的48%；部分伪造场景中严格准确率仅9%，且77%的情况下听众将合成句误判为真实；人类和高斯声音检测器在失败模式上互补，都难以可靠定位短篡改；同时听众对真实音频的误判率上升。

Conclusion: 在现代和部分伪造条件下，人类感知单独不足以可靠检测deepfake音频，需要程序化验证、溯源、水印和段级检测等辅助手段来保障音频内容的真实性。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [126] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出COPA框架，将LLM的提示注入防御建模为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和边界加权经验回放实现持续偏好优化，使模型能不断适应新攻击同时不遗忘旧防御，相比SOTA方法将攻击成功率降低最多6.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御提示注入攻击的方法多为静态的，依赖固定的对齐目标或攻击特定过滤机制，无法应对持续演化的对抗性攻击。虽然近期终身对齐方法处理了用户偏好变化，但未考虑会不断利用既有防御弱点进行适应的恶意攻击者。在真实部署中，攻击分布不断演化，亟需既能持续适应新威胁、又能保持对旧攻击鲁棒性的防御框架。

Method: COPA将提示注入防御转化为终身学习问题。具体而言：(1) 采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）优化，从新观察到的攻击中增量地整合反馈信号进行持续对齐；(2) 设计边界加权经验回放（margin-weighted experience replay）机制，选择性地保留并重放对先前攻击类别的防御经验，以缓解灾难性遗忘；(3) 在持续适应新威胁的同时保持模型通用能力不被破坏。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流实验中，COPA相比现有最先进的防御方法，将攻击成功率平均降低4.4倍，最大降低6.3倍，同时有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并保持了通用模型能力。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化是防御LLM对抗性攻击者的有效范式。COPA证明了将防御建模为终身学习问题、通过增量优化和经验回放结合的方式，能够在不断演化的威胁环境中实现持续鲁棒性，为LLM安全对齐提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [127] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoCoT攻击方法，通过API交互从黑盒大型推理模型（LRMs）中近乎逐字提取隐藏思维链（CoT）。在开源模型上达到最高66.4%的近乎逐字提取成功率，在Gemini-2.5上成功提取33463个token的长CoT，证明了隐藏CoT提取是现实的安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大型推理模型的隐藏思维链轨迹是珍贵的模型资产，但此前尚未系统探究是否可通过API交互从黑盒模型中直接提取这些隐藏CoT。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估隐藏CoT作为安全资产面临的实际风险。

Method: 作者识别出工具调用之间的"推理重放表面"（reasoning replay surface），开发了多步攻击方法EchoCoT，利用API返回的保真度信号迭代提取隐藏CoT；同时提出基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索跨数据集有效的通用注入轨迹（universal injection trajectory）。

Result: 在三个开源和五个前沿专有LRM上评估：开源模型上EchoCoT达到最高66.4%的近乎逐字提取成功率，提取轨迹长度与目标相差不超过10%，至少90%的token与目标CoT完全匹配；同一注入轨迹可泛化至未见数据集，最高达80%提取成功率；专有模型中大量提取的CoT与供应商报告的推理长度及可用CoT摘要高度吻合；在Gemini-2.5上成功提取33463个token（目标32948个token）。

Conclusion: 隐藏CoT提取已被证明是实际可行的安全风险，当前黑盒LRMs对隐藏推理过程的保护不足，亟需加强对隐藏CoT资产的安全防护。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [128] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识协议让领域专家对文档进行认证，利用安全多方计算聚合信任分数，并通过跨链哈希承诺确保文档和分数的不可篡改与可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，缺乏文档来源验证、篡改检测和信任评估机制。在医疗、金融、物流和法律等对检索内容时效性和准确性要求极高的领域，错误或被篡改的文档可能直接导致决策失误，因此亟需一个可验证、可信赖的RAG框架。

Method: TrustRAG采用委员会式认证机制：文档在使用前由领域专家委员会通过零知识协议进行认证，专家的隐藏评分通过安全多方计算（MPC）聚合成可验证的信任分数。文档数据和信任分数通过哈希承诺在跨链间联合维护，确保任何文档或分数无法被静默篡改或删除，所有排序均可独立重放验证。

Result: 该论文提出TrustRAG系统框架，实现了文档认证、信任评分聚合和跨链数据完整性保障，使RAG系统的每次检索排序都能被独立回放和检查，从根本上解决文档来源可信度和内容完整性问题。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过结合区块链、零知识证明和安全多方计算技术，为RAG系统提供了端到端的信任保障机制，在高风险领域（如医疗、金融等）具有重要应用价值，能够有效防止错误或篡改文档导致的不良决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [129] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法（分析13个会议的征稿启事、系统检索2016-2025年间24篇复现研究、使用Olszewski等人提出的复现分类法进行归类、并对作者进行问卷调查）系统考察了可用安全与隐私领域中复现研究的现状，揭示了四个关键见解，并为会议、研究者和审稿人提出了增强复现实践的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用安全与隐私研究领域仍处于发展初期，在复现研究方面缺乏成熟的标准和规范。作者旨在深入了解该领域复现研究的当前状况，以便为该领域建立更清晰的复现实践指南提供依据。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 检查13个涵盖安全、隐私和人机交互领域的会议征稿启事；(2) 在2016至2025年间对这些会议中的复现用户研究论文进行系统检索，最终获得24篇相关论文；(3) 使用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复现分类法对这24篇论文进行分类；(4) 向这些论文的作者分发调查问卷，以了解其开展复现研究的动机。

Result: 分析揭示了四个关键发现：(A) 征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复现工作指南；(B) 阅读复现论文时很难确定相对于原始研究做了哪些修改；(C) 样本中不存在严格的完全重复研究，约三分之二的研究修改了原作的多个方面；(D) 影响结果的时间和情境变化是最常被引用的复现动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，作者为会议主办方、研究者和同行评审者提供了切实可行的建议，以加强可用安全与隐私研究领域的复现实践。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [130] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，用于在秘密共享数据上训练和推理CellCnn卷积神经网络，实现对稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的隐私保护检测，在保持接近明文精度性能的同时超越先前隐私保护基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维单细胞测量中稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的检测对理解白血病和病毒感染等疾病至关重要。CellCnn已能识别频率低至0.01%的细胞群体，但其可靠训练需要比任何单一机构所能提供更大、更多样化的患者队列，而单细胞数据过于敏感，无法在现有隐私法规下跨机构共享。因此需要一种既能实现协作训练又能保护患者数据隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个安全多方计算（MPC）框架，使CellCnn的完整训练和推理过程在秘密共享（secret-shared）数据上执行。框架确保参与方和计算服务器都无法观察到原始患者数据或中间值。与早期方法不同，该框架保留CellCnn架构中的ReLU激活函数和偏置项等关键组件，而非将其移除。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染（CMV）和急性髓系白血病（AML）的基准单细胞数据集上进行了评估，实现了接近明文（plaintext）对应版本的精度，同时优于先前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该MPC框架在保留CellCnn完整架构组件（ReLU和偏置）的前提下，实现了接近明文精度的隐私保护训练与推理，为跨机构协作分析敏感单细胞数据提供了可行方案，无需暴露原始患者数据。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [131] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于解决移动边缘网络中缓存卸载面临的恶意节点和自私行为问题，通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，结合激励机制和认证机制，在考虑节点移动性、能耗和算力约束下优化信任、缓存容量和成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量不断增长，边缘服务器内容缓存对降低延迟至关重要，但存在恶意边缘服务器（可能删除或篡改缓存内容）和边缘服务器容量有限两大挑战。为克服容量限制可借助辅助移动节点贡献缓存资源，但辅助节点存在自私行为（需要激励机制）和恶意行为（需要信任管理）的双重问题。

Method: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制：(1) 利用智能合约计算直接信任和间接信任，准确过滤恶意节点；(2) 以移动节点对服务质量的满意度作为可信度判定依据，信任数据安全存储在区块链上；(3) 引入奖励机制激励缓存资源共享以对抗节点自私性；(4) 设计基于区块链的认证机制抵御节点冒充攻击；(5) 在共识过程中考虑移动节点移动性、能耗和算力约束，优化信任、缓存容量和成本效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在数据含有10%噪声的情况下，所提方法仍能准确区分诚实服务器和恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 该区块链驱动的信任管理机制能够有效识别可信的边缘服务器和移动节点，解决恶意行为与自私行为带来的缓存卸载问题，在存在数据噪声的复杂环境下仍保持较高的识别准确性。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [132] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形（many-to-many randomized traffic morphing）的鲁棒网站指纹（WF）防御方法，通过在Tor可插拔传输（PT）场景中使用radix-trie同步机制实现带宽和时间开销的大幅降低，并在多个数据集和攻击场景下显著优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF防御大多存在可学习的网页流量映射特征；此外，对抗训练下的鲁棒性并不一定意味着对防御感知自编码器（DAAE）攻击的鲁棒性，因此需要更强的防御方案。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形策略，选择具有高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选；随机将每个网页流量映射到多个候选目标，并允许多个不同网页共享变形目标以增加对抗不确定性。为支持实际Tor部署，引入基于radix-trie的同步机制，利用数据包方向前缀实现PT端点的一致变形流量识别，并结合流量突变和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的封闭与开放世界场景中，对抗六种最先进防御和五种WF攻击：相比Adaptive Tamaraw，Chameleon将对抗训练攻击准确率降低最多36.74%，带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%；在GTT23数据集上的DAAE攻击下，将攻击F1-score限制在35.19%（Adaptive Tamaraw仅为88.22%）；真实PT桥接评估中仅增加16.25%时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机映射和高效同步机制，在显著降低开销的同时有效抵御包括DAAE在内的高级WF攻击，是一种实用且鲁棒的Tor WF防御方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>
